Bulldog\n![Placeholder Image](https://tellmeajoke.today/wp-content/plugins/testimonial-pro/src/Frontend/assets/images/placeholder-image.png)\n##### Tell Me A Joke\n15/02/2025\n**\n**\n## **One Person Can Make A Difference.**\nHi, My Name is Mark Rogers, Australian born, living in hills of Adelaide.\nThe idea for the***Tell Me A Joke***website came to me when I was**Depressed**, searching the web for a bit of light hearted material to**pick up my spirits.**\nSure, there were some joke sites, but they were static, not being updated, and surely in the world of jokes and inspiration, there must be more than &#8220;100 of the best jokes&#8221;.\n**Jokes and Inspiration Are Very Personal,**what works for one person doesn&#8217;t work for another. Which reduces how many jokes and inspirational messages actually make an impact. So that 100 could easily drop to 25.\nBelieve it or not, I am no comedian, nor oracle, nor do I know all the jokes in the world, and I am not particularly wise. take the time to make sure the submission is not already in the database to make sure its new, and publish it.\nOver time this will enable the site to become the**&#8216;One Stop Shop&#8217;**for jokes and inspiration.\nSo please join me on this journey,**[share a joke or drop some wisdom](https://tellmeajoke.today/share-your-joke-inspiration/),**and let&#8217;s**Make The World A Little But we're here to lighten the mood and put a smile on your face with the best funny jokes around! Share them with your friends, coworkers, and even your kids: You don't have to worry about these being clean. All of our[favorite jokes](https://www.countryliving.com/favorite-jokes/)are fit for kids and adults. In addition to the 125 jokes below, we've also got[dad jokes](https://www.countryliving.com/life/a27452412/best-dad-jokes/), jokes for[kiddos](https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a30756797/jokes-for-kids/),[mom jokes](https://www.countryliving.com/life/a32404390/mom-jokes/), and jokes for[holidays](https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a30502343/valentines-day-jokes/)that you can share them with the youngest person in the room (be sure to bookmark our[April Fool's jokes](https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a43315531/best-april-fools-day-jokes/)for next year!)\nGet ready: Some of what's to come is quite punny. Some might even make your eyes roll. But, deep down, if we are honest, who doesn't smile at[corny jokes](https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a38697531/corny-jokes/)? Others might even make you laugh so hard you cry, so don't say we didn't warn you. Many are one-liners so you can remember them to share and share again, and your kids can retell them to their friends too, maybe even years later. Now get ready to make some memories filled with laughter with these 125 hilariously funny jokes!\n###### More Jokes to Make Them Laugh\n* [![](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/dad-jokes-661d7f3db58e2.jpg?crop=1.00xw:0.752xh;0,0.0625xh&amp;resize=300:* \"Father and daughter wearing robot costumes at home\")Dad Jokes to Keep the Whole Family Laughing](https://www.countryliving.com/life/a27452412/best-dad-jokes/)\n* [![](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/childrenlaughing-66841ed582351.jpg?crop=1.00xw:0.752xh;0,0&amp;resize=300:* \"two young girls laughing in the backyard\")200 Funny Jokes for Kids That You'll Laugh at Too](https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a30756797/jokes-for-kids/)\n* [![](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/kid-and-doctor-making-silly-faces-672255db2d4af.jpg?crop=1.00xw:0.752xh;0,0.00962xh&amp;resize=300:* \"a young blonde haired boy holding a stuffed teddy bear sticks out his tongue and makes silly faces with his doctor\")165 Knock-Knock Jokes That'll Leave Them Howling](https://www.countryliving.com/life/kids-pets/a36176164/knock-knock-jokes/)\n* What did one snowman say to the other snowman? It smells like carrots over here!\n* Why did Beethoven get rid of his chickens? All they ever said was, “Bach, Bach, Bach!”\n* What did 20 do when it was hungry? Twenty-eight.\n* Why is grass so dangerous? Because it's full of blades!\n* Why are mountains so funny? They’re hill areas.\n* Why wasn’t the cactus invited to hang out with the mushrooms? He wasn’t a fungi.\n* Why shouldn’t you fundraise for marathons? They just take the money and run.\n* Why did the crab cross the road? It didn’t—it used the sidewalk.\n* Why does it take pirates a long time to learn the alphabet? Because they can spend years at C!\n* Why can't a nose be 12 inches long? Because then it would be a foot.\n* Why can’t you put two half-dollars in your pocket? Because two halves make a*hole,*and your money will fall out!\n* Why does a moon rock taste better than an Earth rock? It’s a little meteor.\n* How much do rainbows weigh? Not much. They’re actually pretty light.\n* What is the most popular fish in the ocean? The starfish.\n* A slice of apple pie costs $2.50 in Jamaica, $3.75 in Bermuda, and $3 in the Bahamas. Those are the pie-rates of the Caribbean.\n* Why did the football coach yell at the vending machine? He wanted his quarter back!\n* I had a joke about paper today, but it was*tear*able.\n* What kind of job can you get at a bicycle factory? A*spokes*person\n* What does a condiment wizard perform? Saucery\n* What's the difference in an alligator and a crocodile? You’ll see one later and one in a while.\n* What’s the difference between the bird flu and the swine flu? One requires*tweetment*and the other an*oinkment.*\n* What’s the difference between ducks and dine-and-dashers? Ducks take care of their bills.\n* What's the difference between spring rolls and summer rolls? Their*season*ing.\n* What’s the difference between Iron Man and Aluminum Man? Iron Man stops the bad guy. Aluminum Man foils their plans.\n* **What’s the difference between a poorly dressed man on a unicycle and a well-dressed man on a bicycle? Attire.\n* What’s the difference between a $20 steak and a $55 steak? February 14th.\n* What's the best thing about Switzerland? The flag is a big plus.\n* I went to the aquarium this weekend, but I didn’t stay long. There’s something fishy about that place.\n* I found a lion in my closet the other day! When I asked what it was doing there, it said “Narnia business.”\n* What's a cat's favorite instrument? Purr-cussion.\n* Why did the snail paint a giant S on his car? So when he drove by, people could say: “Look at that S car go!”\n* What do you call a happy cowboy? A jolly rancher.\n* What subject do cats like best in school? Hiss-tory.\n* Humpty Dumpty had a great fall. He said his summer was pretty good too.\n* My boss said “dress for the job you want, not for the job you have.” So I went in as Batman.\n* How do you make holy water? You boil the hell out of it.\n* Justice is a dish best served cold. Otherwise, it's just water.\n* Why should you never throw grandpa's false teeth at a vehicle? You might denture car.\n* Why are Christmas trees bad at knitting? They always drop their needles.\n* What did the lunch box say to the refrigerator? Don't hate me because I'm a little cooler.\n* I can always tell when someone is lying. I can tell when they're standing too.\n* Some people pick their nose, but I was born with mine.\n![two nerd boys dressed in casual clothing, glasses and bow ties experiment with a homemade science project they are both smiling and sitting at a table, and one is looking at the other with helmets on their heads in front of a beige background retro styling](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/funny-jokes-kids-662e751fbf77b.jpg?resize=980:* \"Two Boys Dressed as Nerds Smiling with Mind Reading Helmets\")[![pinterest](https://www.countryliving.com/_assets/design-tokens/fre/static/icons/social/pinterest.e8cf655.svg?primary=%2523ffffff)](https://pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?url=https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a36178514/hilariously-funny-jokes/&amp;description=countryliving&amp;media=https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/funny-jokes-kids-662e751fbf77b.jpg?resize=1600:*)\nRichVintage\n* What's the coolest element in the periodic table? Digest\n# 171 Bad Jokes That You Can’t Help but Laugh At\n[]()![171 Bad Jokes That You Can’t Help but Laugh At](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-53.jpg)\nReader&#039;s Digest\nBy[Caroline Fanning](https://www.rd.com/author/caroline-fanning/)\nUpdated onMay 07, 2025\n* []()\n* []()\n* []()\n* []()\nSome bad jokes deserve only eye rolls and groans. But somehow, these manage to be truly funny.\n## Bad jokes that are actually pretty good\nAh, dumb jokes. They’re little guilty pleasures we indulge in with giddy enthusiasm every chance we get. They make us groan and say, “Are you serious?” But, of course, they also make us chuckle.**Bad jokes**can be[short](https://www.rd.com/list/short-jokes/), corny or punny, and some of them deliver the best one-liners ever. What’s not to love?\nIf you’re a sucker for a good bad joke, you’re in luck. We’ve rounded up the[funniest jokes](https://www.rd.com/list/funniest-jokes-of-all-time/)that just so happen to pack groan-worthy punch lines. These stupid jokes touch on a range of topics, from animals and food to science and ghosts. Some might make you laugh, while others might have you running for the actually funny ones. Either way, you’ve been warned!\nRead on for our roundup of bad jokes—they’re so terrible, they loop back around to being hilarious.\n**Get*Reader’s******Digest*****’s******[**Read Up newsletter**](https://www.rd.com/newsletter/)****for more humor, cleaning, travel, tech and fun facts all week long.**\n1/172\n![Knock knock.](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## Knock, knock.\n*Who’s there?*\nInterrupting[cow](https://www.rd.com/article/animal-jokes/).\n*Interrupting c—*\nMOO!\n2/172\n![If you](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-2.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## If you’re American when you go in the bathroom ……and American when you come out, what are you in the bathroom?\nEuropean.\n3/172\n![What did the fish say when he swam into a wall?](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-3.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## What did the fish say when he swam into a wall?\nDam.\n4/172\n![What do you call a fish with no eyes?](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-4.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## What do you call a fish with no eyes?\nA fsh.\n5/172\n![What do you call a can opener that doesn’t work?](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-5.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## What do you call a can opener that doesn’t work?\nA can’t opener!\n6/172\n \n![What do you get when you combine a rhetorical question and a joke?](https://www.rd.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/01/175BadJokes-6.jpg?fit=700%2C700)Reader's Digest\n## What do you get when you combine a rhetorical question and a joke?\n…Get it? the author of*Now That’s Funny.*We’ve earned prestigious ASME awards for our humor—including comical quips, pranks, puns, cartoons, one-liners, knock-knock jokes, riddles, memes, tweets and stories in Which reduces how many jokes and inspirational messages actually make an impact. So that 100 could easily drop to 25.\nBelieve it or not, I am no comedian, nor oracle, nor do I know all the jokes in the world, and I am not particularly wise. But**Do You Know Who Has All The Jokes And Inspiration**in the world?**It&#8217;s The World!**\nThis is why anyone can[**share a joke or**](https://tellmeajoke.today/tell-your-joke-inspiration/)**[inspirational](https://tellmeajoke.today/share-your-joke-inspiration/)**[**quote/story.**](https://tellmeajoke.today/tell-your-joke-inspiration/)\nI take the time to make sure the submission is not already in the database to make sure its new, and publish it.\nOver time this will enable the site to become the**&#8216;One Stop Shop&#8217;**for jokes and inspiration.\nSo please join me on this journey,**[share a joke or drop some wisdom](https://tellmeajoke.today/share-your-joke-inspiration/),**and let&#8217;s**Make The World A Little A four-chin teller.\n![high angle view of cheerful grandparents talking with granddaughters while sitting on sofa at home](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-family-1658947911.jpg?resize=980:* \"Cheerful grandparents enjoying with granddaughters\")[![pinterest](https://www.countryliving.com/_assets/design-tokens/fre/static/icons/social/pinterest.e8cf655.svg?primary=%2523ffffff)](https://pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?url=https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a36178514/hilariously-funny-jokes/&amp;description=countryliving&amp;media=https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-family-1658947911.jpg?resize=1600:*)\nKlaus Vedfelt\n* What do you get when you mix a cocker spaniel, a poodle, and a ghost? A cocker-poodle boo.\n* How do celebrities stay cool? They have many fans.\n* What does a pickle say when he wants to play cards? &quot;Dill me in!&quot;\n* How much money does a pirate pay for corn? A buccaneer.\n* Where do young trees go to learn? Elementree school.\n* Why do bees have sticky hair? Because they use a honeycomb.\n* How did the student feel when he learned about electricity? Totally shocked.\n* What do you call a bee that can't make up its mind? A Maybe.\n* Why was six afraid of seven? Because 7-8-9.\n* If April showers bring May flowers, what do May flowers bring? Pilgrims.\n* I tried to catch fog yesterday. Mist.\n* What do you call a hippie's wife? Mississippi.\n* Two peanuts were walking down the street. One was a-salted.\n* How can you tell it’s a dogwood tree? By the bark.\n* What did the buffalo say when his kid went to college? Bison.\n* What did the mayonnaise say when the refrigerator door was opened? Close the door, I'm dressing.\n* What's the stinkiest planet? Poopiter.\n![two best friends telling secrets lying in the grass](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-friends-1658948000.jpg?resize=980:* \"Two best friends telling secrets lying in the grass\")[![pinterest](https://www.countryliving.com/_assets/design-tokens/fre/static/icons/social/pinterest.e8cf655.svg?primary=%2523ffffff)](https://pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?url=https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a36178514/hilariously-funny-jokes/&amp;description=countryliving&amp;media=https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-friends-1658948000.jpg?resize=1600:*)\nWestend61\n* What did one wall say to the other? I'll meet you at the corner.\n* Why don't sharks eat clowns? Because they taste funny.\n* A horse walks into a bar. The bartender says, &quot;Why the long face?&quot;\n* What did the pirate say when he turned 80? Aye matey.\n* What's black and white and goes round and round? A penguin in the washing machine.\n* How do you organize a space party? You planet.\n* Why couldn't the bicycle stand up by itself? It was two tired.\n* Did you hear the rumor about butter? Well, I'm not going to spread it.\n* Why did the student eat his homework? Because his teacher told him it was a piece of cake.\n* What did one hat say to the other? You wait here, I'll go on ahead.\n* What do you call a dinosaur that crashes his car? Tyrannosaurus Wrecks.\n* I couldn't figure out why the baseball kept getting larger. Then it hit me.\n* What do you call a boomerang that won’t come back? A stick.\n* What did the full glass say to the empty glass? You look drunk.\n* How do you stop a bull from charging? Cancel its credit card.\n* Why don’t we see elephants hiding in trees? Because they’re really good at it.\n![happy multiracial senior women having fun together outdoor elderly generation people hugging each other at park](https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-women-1658948150.jpg?resize=980:* \"Happy multiracial senior women having fun together outdoor - Elderly generation people hugging each other at park\")[![pinterest](https://www.countryliving.com/_assets/design-tokens/fre/static/icons/social/pinterest.e8cf655.svg?primary=%2523ffffff)](https://pinterest.com/pin/create/button/?url=https://www.countryliving.com/life/entertainment/a36178514/hilariously-funny-jokes/&amp;description=countryliving&amp;media=https://hips.hearstapps.com/hmg-prod/images/hilarious-jokes-women-1658948150.jpg?resize=1600:*)\nSabrina Bracher\n* What’s the difference between a hippo and a zippo? One’s pretty heavy and the other’s a little lighter.\n* Did you hear the one about the roof? Never mind, it's over your head.\n* I used to hate facial hair...but then it grew on me.\n* A cheese factory exploded in France. Da brie was everywhere.\n* What's a ninja's favorite type of shoes? Which reduces how many jokes and inspirational messages actually make an impact. So that 100 could easily drop to 25.\nBelieve it or not, I am no comedian, nor oracle, nor do I know all the jokes in the world, and I am not particularly wise. But**Do You Know Who Has All The Jokes And Inspiration**in the world?**It&#8217;s The World!**\nThis is why anyone can[**share a joke or**](https://tellmeajoke.today/tell-your-joke-inspiration/)**[inspirational](https://tellmeajoke.today/share-your-joke-inspiration/)**[**quote/story.**](https://tellmeajoke.today/tell-your-joke-inspiration/)\nI take the time to make sure the submission is not already in the database to make sure its new, and publish it.\nOver time this will enable the site to become the**&#8216;One Stop Shop&#8217;**for jokes and inspiration.\nSo please join me on this journey,**[share a joke or drop some wisdom](https://tellmeajoke.today/share-your-joke-inspiration/),**and let&#8217;s**Make The World A Little Better**for those that need it.\nBe that**One Person**and**Make A Difference!**\n### **[Tell Me A Joke](https://tellmeajoke.today/)**\n### **[PowerME Wealth System](https://powerme.au)**\n###
